What Anton (the client) ordered, and what the three rounds since the last review (R19, R20, R21) did:
- Anton called the earlier blue -> drawing transition "extremely flat" and asked for "a fine transition with a scroll effect". The previous review (r18) judged it "too restrained": 1px lines at 2.86:1 contrast on the blue, and the horizontal landing line was never drawn (a CSS specificity bug rendered it 1x1px).
- R19 ("Blåkopian arket", plan 062): the blue plate no longer just lifts — it SHRINKS, scrubbed on scroll, from a full screen down to exactly the drawing sheet's rectangle (clip-path on --tx), its fill cross-fading toward the drafting-table tone, and a 2px white contour fading in late (tx 0.6-0.9) so the sheet reads as if it SOLIDIFIES. The construction lines (sheet edges + the two stations) are a second, coordinated layer on the same --tx clock. The landing-line specificity bug was fixed (.seam .seam-h), sheet edges and the landing line are now 2px, the stations stay 1px, and --seam-on-blue was corrected to pure white. Hōrai's iframe now mounts earlier, so the Calyx -> Hōrai effect no longer freezes. "SEO-optimering" got a non-breaking hyphen.
- R20 and R21: an attempt to make the landed contour sheet re-land as position:fixed and take step 01's place was tried and then REMOVED again. The accepted behaviour now is: the contour sheet lands, and then simply scrolls out of the viewport with the chapter, and step 01's own sticky sheet arrives later. TWO SEPARATE SHEETS, with a gap of empty ground between them, is the lead's accepted decision. Do NOT propose re-introducing a freeze, a position:fixed hand-over, or a morph between the two sheets; if the gap costs the page something, say what it costs and name a move that does not reintroduce a hand-over.

  437:   /* Kortet sjÃ¤lvt. Rotationen rÃ¤knas i ren CSS ur --sc-ps:
  438:        t    = clamp(0, (ps - 0.10) / 0.68, 1)
  439:        flip = t*t*(3 - 2t)                         (smoothstep 0.10 -> 0.78)
  440:      ps 0 till 0,10: flip 0, kortet stilla medan ljuspÃ¶len vandrar.
  441:      ps 0,10 till 0,78: vÃ¤ndningen.
  442:      ps 0,78 till 1: flip 1, baksidan i vila, dwell lÃ¥ter den landa.
  443:      BÃ¥da riktningarna, eftersom hela uttrycket Ã¤r en funktion av ps.

  445:      0,10-0,78. VÃ¤ndningen tar 1 469 px vid 1440x900 (0,68 x 2 160), svansen
  446:      (baksidans vila + dwell) 655 px, mot tidigare 2 052 px + 1 273 px
  447:      stillastÃ¥ende (scroll_verify: band 2700-3300 diff 0,0). */
  448:   .plate-card{position:absolute;inset:0;
  449:     --t:clamp(0,calc((var(--sc-ps,0) - 0.10) / 0.68),1);
  450:     --flip:calc(var(--t) * var(--t) * (3 - 2 * var(--t)));
  451:     transform-style:preserve-3d;

 2672:   function snapCaseWrap(C){
 2673:     var wrap = C.el.querySelector('.case-wrap');
 2674:     if (!wrap) return;
 2675:     var dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
 2676:     var rect = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
 2677:     if (!rect.height) return;
 2678:     var snapped = Math.round(rect.height * dpr) / dpr;
 2679:     if (Math.abs(snapped - rect.height) > 0.02){
 2680:       wrap.style.flex = 'none';
 2681:       wrap.style.height = snapped.toFixed(3) + 'px';
 2682:     } else {
 2683:       wrap.style.flex = '';
 2684:       wrap.style.height = '';
 2685:     }
 2686:   }
 2687:   function measureCase(C){
 2688:     snapCaseWrap(C);
 2689:     var f = C.el.querySelector('iframe'), w = f && f.contentWindow, d = f && f.contentDocument;
 2690:     if (!d || !d.documentElement) return;
 2691:     var ih = w.innerHeight, max = d.documentElement.scrollHeight - ih;
 2692:     C.f = f; C.w = w; C.y = C.anchors.map(function(a){
 2693:       var el = a[0] === 'top' ? null : d.querySelector(a[0]);
 2694:       var t = el ? el.offsetTop : 0;
 2695:       var off = a[1] === 'center' ? (el ? (el.offsetHeight - ih) / 2 : 0) : a[1] * ih;
 2696:       if (a[2] && ih < (a[3] || Infinity)) off += a[2];
 2697:       return Math.max(0, Math.min(max, Math.round(t + off)));
 2698:     });
 2699:   }
 2700:   function caseY(C, p){
 2701:     var n = C.y.length, k = Math.min(n - 1, Math.floor(p * n)), t = p * n - k;
 2702:     if (k === 0) return C.y[0];
 2703:     var u = Math.min(1, t / 0.45); u = u * u * (3 - 2 * u);
 2704:     return C.y[k - 1] + (C.y[k] - C.y[k - 1]) * u;
 2705:   }

 2707:   /* PLAN 019, R6, device-tabellens rad 9. --sc-ps som DRIVER ritningen
 2708:      kommer frÃ¥n motorn (#process har data-sc-act="flow"); den hÃ¤r lÃ¤saren

 2711:   function processPs(){
 2712:     var travel = processH + window.innerHeight;
 2713:     if (travel <= 0) return 0;
 2714:     var ps = (window.scrollY + window.innerHeight - processTop) / travel;
 2715:     if (ps < 0) ps = 0; else if (ps > 1) ps = 1;
 2716:     return ps;
 2717:   }

 2742:   /* ---- ramarna monteras en dryg skÃ¤rm i fÃ¶rvÃ¤g ---------------------------
 2743:      `loading="lazy"` ensamt lÃ¤mnar tidpunkten Ã¥t webblÃ¤saren. En
 2744:      IntersectionObserver med rootMargin 100 % sÃ¤tter src nÃ¤r ramen Ã¤r ungefÃ¤r
 2745:      en skÃ¤rm bort, sÃ¥ den Ã¤r laddad nÃ¤r den nÃ¥r vecket men aldrig vid load.
 2746:      Tre live-ramar monterade vid load Ã¤r det tyngsta en marknadssida kan gÃ¶ra
 2747:      mot sig sjÃ¤lv (performance.md Â§ 2). Observern kopplar bort sig per ram, sÃ¥
 2748:      ingen ram monteras tvÃ¥ gÃ¥nger, och SJÃ„LVA ramen rÃ¶rs aldrig efterÃ¥t. */
 2749:   var frames = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('iframe[data-src]'));
 2750:   if (frames.length){
 2751:     if ('IntersectionObserver' in window){
 2752:       /* SjÃ¤lva src-skrivningen lÃ¤ggs i en IDLE-lucka, inte i den bildruta dÃ¤r
 2753:          observern rÃ¥kar utlÃ¶sa. MÃ„TT I R3: att skriva src direkt i callbacken
 2754:          lade tre enorma toppar mitt i den pinnade akten, tydligt synliga som
 2755:          isolerade spikar i `frame-times.png` medan resten av kurvan lÃ¥g platt.
 2756:          Det Ã¤r precis "a long task under the thumb IS the jank"
 2757:          (performance.md Â§ 1). Observern fÃ¥r fortfarande utlÃ¶sa en dryg skÃ¤rm i
 2758:          fÃ¶rvÃ¤g, sÃ¥ ramen hinner bli klar; det Ã¤r TIDPUNKTEN fÃ¶r det dyra
 2759:          arbetet som flyttas, inte fÃ¶rvarningen. Timeouten gÃ¶r att en sida som
 2760:          aldrig blir idle Ã¤ndÃ¥ monterar ramen. */
 2761:       /* PLAN 026: tvÃ¥ hÃ¥l i 018:s idle-lucka (upp till 600ms). (1) Varje
 2762:          isIntersecting-post som kommer medan data-src Ã¤nnu sitter kvar kÃ¶ar
 2763:          ett nytt mountFrame-anrop; det andra lÃ¤ser data-src=null och
 2764:          skriver f.src=null (navigerar till /null). (2) En ram som lÃ¤mnar
 2765:          40%-zonen innan luckan hunnit montera den monteras Ã¤ndÃ¥ (callbacken
 2766:          ligger redan i kÃ¶n) och stÃ¥r vaken utanfÃ¶r skÃ¤rmen. */
 2767:       var mountFrame = function(f){
 2768:         var s = f.getAttribute('data-src');
 2769:         if (!s) return;
 2770:         f.src = s;
 2771:         f.removeAttribute('data-src');
 2772:         f.__pending = false;
 2773:         if (!f.__visible){ var w = f.closest('.frame-wrap'); if (w) w.classList.add('is-asleep'); }
 2774:         /* PLAN 054 steg 4/12. Casens ramar mÃ¤ts EN gÃ¥ng vid load och vid
 2775:            varje storleksÃ¤ndring i ramens EGNA dokument (same-origin), inte
 2776:            per bildruta - latchTick lÃ¤ser bara C.y (redan uppmÃ¤tt) och
 2777:            C.w.scrollTo. */
 2778:         var C = null;
 2779:         for (var ci = 0; ci < CASES.length; ci++){ if (CASES[ci].el.contains(f)) { C = CASES[ci]; break; } }
 2780:         if (C){
 2781:           f.addEventListener('load', function(){
 2782:             measureCase(C);
 2783:             if (f.contentWindow && f.contentDocument && f.contentDocument.documentElement){
 2784:               new f.contentWindow.ResizeObserver(function(){ measureCase(C); }).observe(f.contentDocument.documentElement);
 2785:             }
 2786:             kickLatch();
 2787:           });
 2788:         }
 2789:       };
 2790:       var defer = window.requestIdleCallback
 2791:         ? function(fn){ requestIdleCallback(fn, { timeout: 600 }); }
 2792:         : function(fn){ setTimeout(fn, 1); };
 2793:       /* PLAN 018, R6. TvÃ¥ separata fel mÃ¤tta av perf-granskaren:
 2794:          (a) rootMargin 100% monterade Calyx-ramen 500 px INNE i
 2795:          kortvÃ¤ndningen (scrollY ~2560, heron p95 435,9ms med ramarna mot
 2796:          66,7ms utan) - 40% monterar fÃ¶rst vid heronts --sc-ps >= 1,0.
 2797:          (b) `obs.unobserve` kopplade bort observern permanent efter
 2798:          montering, sÃ¥ en monterad ram som scrollar ur syn aldrig sÃ¶vdes
 2799:          igen; Calyx/HÅrai kÃ¶r sina egna scrollmotorer pÃ¥ huvudtrÃ¥den
 2800:          resten av sidan. Observern kopplas nu ALDRIG bort: en ram utanfÃ¶r
 2801:          40% fÃ¥r .is-asleep (content-visibility:hidden) pÃ¥ sin wrapper,
 2802:          en ram innanfÃ¶r vaknar och monteras (om den inte redan har src). */
 2803:       /* PLAN 027: observern satt pÃ¥ RAMEN, men sÃ¶mnen sÃ¤tts pÃ¥ WRAPPERN
 2804:          (.is-asleep). Ett element inne i ett content-visibility:hidden-
 2805:          subtrÃ¤d har ingen box, sÃ¥ nÃ¤sta gÃ¥ng observern rapporterar den
 2806:          (t.ex. en tidigare post i samma batch redan sÃ¶vt wrappern) lÃ¤ses
 2807:          ramen som 0x0/inte-korsande fÃ¶r alltid - ramen somnade permanent
 2808:          fÃ¶rsta gÃ¥ngen wrappern sÃ¶vdes, oavsett var den faktiskt stod.
 2809:          Observern observerar nu wrappern (alltid en box, min-height stÃ¥r)

**A) Pair verdicts**

Measurements below refer to our viewport, excluding the comparison caption.

1. **Holds the reference’s level.** The entering blue surface already occupies approximately 39% of the screen.
2. **Holds the reference’s level.** Insetting the plate makes the scroll effect spatially readable.
3. **Holds the reference’s level.** The changing plate occupies approximately 50% of the viewport area.
4. **Holds the reference’s level.** The landing line develops above the heading without crossing it.

**B) Findings, most material first**

1. **Heading clipping remains possible between the supplied settled captures.** Thesis text, case headings and incoming process bands use continuous inset wipes. **Now fine enough on desktop; no longer too restrained. Mobile still needs coordination.**

The title stays below the landing line in these captures. The remaining contour defect affects finish, not the effect’s overall strength.
